Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   This is an ML research experiment management session for the **trace-verdict-study** project targeting EMNLP 2026 (ARR May 25 deadline, ~10 days remaining). The agent coordinates VOTA (Verdict-Oriented Trace Abstraction) experiments comparing compressed execution traces vs full traces vs no traces for test verdict prediction (pass/fail). The agent acts as an execution coordinator (调度员), spawning Workers for SSH operations, code changes, and analysis, while managing experiment registry, GPU allocation, and Director communication.

   The most recent Director instruction requires:
   1. Re-run evaluate.py on `/vota/final/` checkpoint on cuda:1 with `--save_predictions` to get consistent eval_results.json + predictions.jsonl. Check other Qwen conditions for similar overwrite issues.
   2. Run `eval_qwen_epochs.sh` on cuda:1 for Qwen epoch-level accuracy data.
   3. Update registry: vota_seed42_v5 accuracy → 99.41%, deepseek_exception_only_seed42 → success with accuracy 99.33%.
   4. After cuda:1 eval tasks complete, prepare calibration_deepseek.sh environment.

2. Key Technical Concepts:

   - Component ablation: exception_only, loop_only, ae_only, vota_no_ae conditions
   - eval_loss vs verdict accuracy decoupling: eval_loss ↑ but accuracy ↑ across epochs
   - load_best_model_at_end revert: using eval_loss for checkpoint selection picks worse classifier
   - Bootstrap pairwise CI: paired (per-sample) vs unpaired methods — must not mix in same table
   - `--save_predictions` flag added to evaluate.py for per-sample output (predictions.jsonl)
   - CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES management with CVD fix guards
   - submit_training_job with tmux backend + cron monitoring
   - Registry state machine: PREPARING → QUEUED → RUNNING → ANALYZING → DONE

4. Errors and fixes:

    "content": {
      "type": "text",
      "file": {
        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/artifacts/deepseek_vota_seed42/report/analysis.md",
        "content": "# DeepSeek VOTA Seed42 实验分析\n\n## 实验配置\n- Model: deepseek-ai/deepseek-coder-6.7b-instruct\n- Condition: vota\n- Data: 4720 train / 1190 test\n- Seed: 42\n- Training: 3 epochs, 444 steps, ~2h12m on cuda:0\n\n## 核心指标\n\n| Metric | Value | 95% CI |\n|--------|-------|--------|\n| Accuracy | 84.45% | [82.60%, 86.39%] |\n| Majority baseline | 71.51% | [69.16%, 73.87%] |\n| Lift | +12.94pp | [+11.09pp, +14.87pp] |\n\n### Per-class metrics\n\n| Class | Precision | Recall | F1 |\n|-------|-----------|--------|-----|\n| pass | 0.821 [0.800, 0.843] | 1.000 [1.000, 1.000] | 0.902 [0.889, 0.915] |\n| fail | 1.000 [1.000, 1.000] | 0.454 [0.405, 0.505] | 0.625 [0.576, 0.671] |\n\nBootstrap: 1000 resamples\n\n## 训练 Loss 曲线\n\n**Train loss（第一个 epoch 采样）：**\n- epoch 0.07: 0.4668 → epoch 0.27: 0.1057 → epoch 0.95: 0.0522\n- 快速收敛，0.3 epoch 内 loss 从 0.47 降到 0.09\n\n**Eval loss & classification accuracy by epoch：**\n| Epoch | Eval Loss | Eval Mean Token Acc | Verdict Accuracy | Fail Recall |\n|-------|-----------|---------------------|-----------------|-------------|\n| 1.0 | 0.0679 | 0.9802 | 78.15% | 23.3% |\n| 2.0 | 0.0950 | 0.9794 | — | — |\n| 3.0 | 0.1121 | 0.9791 | 84.45% | 45.4% |\n\n**eval_loss 与 classification accuracy 解耦**：eval_loss 单调递增 (+65%) 但 verdict accuracy 从 78.15% 提升到 84.45% (+6.3pp)，fail recall 从 23.3% 提升到 45.4% (+22.1pp)。continued training 改善了分类能力（尤其是 fail 识别），eval_loss 上升反映 token-level calibration 退化而非分类能力下降。eval_loss 不是 verdict task 的可靠 checkpoint selection criterion。\n\n### Epoch-level Classification Performance\n| Epoch | Eval Loss | Accuracy | Fail Recall | Fail Precision |\n|-------|-----------|----------|-------------|----------------|\n| 1 | 0.0679 | 78.15% | 23.3% | — |\n| 3 (final) | 0.1121 | 84.45% | 45.4% | 100% |\n\nContinued training improves verdict classification (+6.3pp accuracy, +22.1pp fail recall) despite increasing eval_loss. This validates using the final checkpoint rather than the lowest-loss checkpoint for verdict classification.\n\n## Within-Model 对比（DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B）\n\n| Condition | Accuracy | 95% CI |\n|-----------|----------|--------|\n| DeepSeek no_trace | 73.4% | [70.8%, 75.7%] |\n| **DeepSeek VOTA** | **84.5%** | **[82.6%, 86.4%]** |\n| **Within-model lift** | **+11.09pp** | CI 不重叠，显著 |\n\n- deepseek_no_trace conclusion = **negative**（lift +1.85pp over majority, CI [-0.93, +4.63] 跨 0，不显著）\n- DeepSeek VOTA within-model lift: 84.45% − 73.36% = **+11.09pp**，统计显著\n\n### Cross-Model Comparison (Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B vs DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B)\n\n| Condition | Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B | DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B | Δ |\n|-----------|-------------------|---------------------|---|\n| no_trace | 75.1% [72.8, 77.5] | 73.4% [70.8, 75.7] | −1.7pp（CI 重叠，不显著）|\n| VOTA | 99.2% [98.6, 99.7] | 84.5% [82.6, 86.4] | −14.7pp（显著）|\n| VOTA within-model lift | +24.0pp | +11.1pp | — |\n\n**核心发现**：两模型 no_trace 基线接近（Δ = −1.7pp，不显著）→ VOTA 效果差距 (−14.7pp) 完全来自 trace 利用效率差异，而非基础代码理解能力差距。\n\n## 关键发现\n\n1. **Pass 偏向严重**：模型对 pass 有 100% recall 但对 fail 只有 45.4% recall，说明模型倾向于预测 pass。Fail precision 为 1.0 表示当模型预测 fail 时总是对的，但它漏掉了超过一半的 fail 样本。\n2. **eval_loss 与 verdict accuracy 解耦**：eval loss 从 epoch 1 的 0.068 增加到 epoch 3 的 0.112 (+65%)，但 verdict accuracy 从 78.15% 提升到 84.45% (+6.3pp)，fail recall 从 23.3% 提升到 45.4% (+22.1pp)。eval_loss 上升反映 token-level calibration 退化，不是分类能力下降。这验证了 revert load_best_model_at_end 的决策正确性——用 eval_loss 选 checkpoint 会选到分类能力更差的 epoch 1。\n3. **VOTA 对 DeepSeek 有效但 trace 利用效率远低于 Qwen**：DeepSeek VOTA within-model lift +11.09pp（vs DeepSeek no_trace 73.4%），而 Qwen VOTA lift +24.0pp。两模型 no_trace 基线接近（Δ=1.7pp，不显著），VOTA 效果差距完全来自 trace 利用效率差异。\n\n## 结论\n\n**Success** — accuracy 84.5% 显著高于 DeepSeek no_trace baseline 73.4%（within-model lift +11.09pp，95% CI 不重叠），VOTA 条件对 DeepSeek 模型同样有效。但 within-model lift (+11.1pp) 远低于 Qwen (+24.0pp)，且两模型 no_trace 基线接近（Δ=1.7pp，不显著），说明 trace 利用效率是跨模型效果差异的主因。",
        "numLines": 79,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 79
      }

### User

_source line 1504 · 2026-05-15T15:12:10.522Z_

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   This is an ML research experiment management session for the **trace-verdict-study** project targeting EMNLP 2026 (ARR May 25 deadline, ~10 days remaining). The agent coordinates VOTA (Verdict-Oriented Trace Abstraction) experiments comparing compressed execution traces vs full traces vs no traces for test verdict prediction (pass/fail) on two models: Qwen2.5-Coder-7B and DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B-Instruct. The agent acts as execution coordinator (调度员), spawning Workers for SSH operations, code changes, and analysis, while managing experiment registry, GPU allocation, and Director communication.

   Key Director instructions this session (chronologically):
   - Submit deepseek_full_trace_seed42 on cuda:0 (completed)
   - Fix DeepSeek pretraining data error ("1.17T" → "2T tokens"), mark cross-model explanation as hypothesis
   - Fix epoch 1 eval "过拟合" (overfitting) conclusion → eval_loss/accuracy decoupling finding
   - Record bootstrap method consistency issue (paired vs unpaired) as TODO
   - Three parallel tasks: GPU status confirmation, calibration script prep, Qwen epoch eval script prep
   - Fix Qwen eval script KeyError bug
   - **Most recent**: Execute eval tasks on freed cuda:1, update registry for completed experiments

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - VOTA trace compression for SFT verdict prediction (pass/fail binary classification)
   - Cross-model validation: Qwen2.5-Coder-7B vs DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B-Instruct
   - eval_loss vs verdict accuracy decoupling: eval_loss ↑ but accuracy ↑ across epochs (key paper finding)
   - Bootstrap CI methods: paired (needs per-sample predictions) vs unpaired (wider CI, conservative)
   - `--save_predictions` flag added to evaluate.py for per-sample prediction output
   - load_best_model_at_end validation: eval_loss-based checkpoint selection picks WORSE classifier
   - Checkpoint overwrite issue: multiple training runs sharing same output directory
   - submit_training_job with tmux backend + cron monitoring for experiment lifecycle
   - Registry state machine: PREPARING → QUEUED → RUNNING → ANALYZING → DONE
   - Resource saturation constraints: GPU idle = 失职, Worker pool < 2 = 失职

3. Files and Code Sections:

{
  "parentUuid": "55cb76ef-cf54-4855-bc94-1fc575553b91",
  "isSidechain": false,
  "attachment": {
    "type": "file",
    "filename": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/artifacts/deepseek_vota_seed42/report/analysis.md",
    "content": {
      "type": "text",
      "file": {
        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/artifacts/deepseek_vota_seed42/report/analysis.md",
        "content": "# DeepSeek VOTA Seed42 实验分析\n\n## 实验配置\n- Model: deepseek-ai/deepseek-coder-6.7b-instruct\n- Condition: vota\n- Data: 4720 train / 1190 test\n- Seed: 42\n- Training: 3 epochs, 444 steps, ~2h12m on cuda:0\n\n## 核心指标\n\n| Metric | Value | 95% CI |\n|--------|-------|--------|\n| Accuracy | 84.45% | [82.60%, 86.39%] |\n| Majority baseline | 71.51% | [69.16%, 73.87%] |\n| Lift | +12.94pp | [+11.09pp, +14.87pp] |\n\n### Per-class metrics\n\n| Class | Precision | Recall | F1 |\n|-------|-----------|--------|-----|\n| pass | 0.821 [0.800, 0.843] | 1.000 [1.000, 1.000] | 0.902 [0.889, 0.915] |\n| fail | 1.000 [1.000, 1.000] | 0.454 [0.405, 0.505] | 0.625 [0.576, 0.671] |\n\nBootstrap: 1000 resamples\n\n## 训练 Loss 曲线\n\n**Train loss（第一个 epoch 采样）：**\n- epoch 0.07: 0.4668 → epoch 0.27: 0.1057 → epoch 0.95: 0.0522\n- 快速收敛，0.3 epoch 内 loss 从 0.47 降到 0.09\n\n**Eval loss & classification accuracy by epoch：**\n| Epoch | Eval Loss | Eval Mean Token Acc | Verdict Accuracy | Fail Recall |\n|-------|-----------|---------------------|-----------------|-------------|\n| 1.0 | 0.0679 | 0.9802 | 78.15% | 23.3% |\n| 2.0 | 0.0950 | 0.9794 | — | — |\n| 3.0 | 0.1121 | 0.9791 | 84.45% | 45.4% |\n\n**eval_loss 与 classification accuracy 解耦**：eval_loss 单调递增 (+65%) 但 verdict accuracy 从 78.15% 提升到 84.45% (+6.3pp)，fail recall 从 23.3% 提升到 45.4% (+22.1pp)。continued training 改善了分类能力（尤其是 fail 识别），eval_loss 上升反映 token-level calibration 退化而非分类能力下降。eval_loss 不是 verdict task 的可靠 checkpoint selection criterion。\n\n### Epoch-level Classification Performance\n| Epoch | Eval Loss | Accuracy | Fail Recall | Fail Precision |\n|-------|-----------|----------|-------------|----------------|\n| 1 | 0.0679 | 78.15% | 23.3% | — |\n| 3 (final) | 0.1121 | 84.45% | 45.4% | 100% |\n\nContinued training improves verdict classification (+6.3pp accuracy, +22.1pp fail recall) despite increasing eval_loss. This validates using the final checkpoint rather than the lowest-loss checkpoint for verdict classification.\n\n## Within-Model 对比（DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B）\n\n| Condition | Accuracy | 95% CI |\n|-----------|----------|--------|\n| DeepSeek no_trace | 73.4% | [70.8%, 75.7%] |\n| **DeepSeek VOTA** | **84.5%** | **[82.6%, 86.4%]** |\n| **Within-model lift** | **+11.09pp** | CI 不重叠，显著 |\n\n- deepseek_no_trace conclusion = **negative**（lift +1.85pp over majority, CI [-0.93, +4.63] 跨 0，不显著）\n- DeepSeek VOTA within-model lift: 84.45% − 73.36% = **+11.09pp**，统计显著\n\n### Cross-Model Comparison (Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B vs DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B)\n\n| Condition | Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B | DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B | Δ |\n|-----------|-------------------|---------------------|---|\n| no_trace | 75.1% [72.8, 77.5] | 73.4% [70.8, 75.7] | −1.7pp（CI 重叠，不显著）|\n| VOTA | 99.2% [98.6, 99.7] | 84.5% [82.6, 86.4] | −14.7pp（显著）|\n| VOTA within-model lift | +24.0pp | +11.1pp | — |\n\n**核心发现**：两模型 no_trace 基线接近（Δ = −1.7pp，不显著）→ VOTA 效果差距 (−14.7pp) 完全来自 trace 利用效率差异，而非基础代码理解能力差距。\n\n## 关键发现\n\n1. **Pass 偏向严重**：模型对 pass 有 100% recall 但对 fail 只有 45.4% recall，说明模型倾向于预测 pass。Fail precision 为 1.0 表示当模型预测 fail 时总是对的，但它漏掉了超过一半的 fail 样本。\n2. **eval_loss 与 verdict accuracy 解耦**：eval loss 从 epoch 1 的 0.068 增加到 epoch 3 的 0.112 (+65%)，但 verdict accuracy 从 78.15% 提升到 84.45% (+6.3pp)，fail recall 从 23.3% 提升到 45.4% (+22.1pp)。eval_loss 上升反映 token-level calibration 退化，不是分类能力下降。这验证了 revert load_best_model_at_end 的决策正确性——用 eval_loss 选 checkpoint 会选到分类能力更差的 epoch 1。\n3. **VOTA 对 DeepSeek 有效但 trace 利用效率远低于 Qwen**：DeepSeek VOTA within-model lift +11.09pp（vs DeepSeek no_trace 73.4%），而 Qwen VOTA lift +24.0pp。两模型 no_trace 基线接近（Δ=1.7pp，不显著），VOTA 效果差距完全来自 trace 利用效率差异。\n\n## 结论\n\n**Success** — accuracy 84.5% 显著高于 DeepSeek no_trace baseline 73.4%（within-model lift +11.09pp，95% CI 不重叠），VOTA 条件对 DeepSeek 模型同样有效。但 within-model lift (+11.1pp) 远低于 Qwen (+24.0pp)，且两模型 no_trace 基线接近（Δ=1.7pp，不显著），说明 trace 利用效率是跨模型效果差异的主因。",
        "numLines": 79,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 79
      }
    },
    "displayPath": "../../../artifacts/deepseek_vota_seed42/report/analysis.md"

1. deepseek_exception_only_seed42 → DONE/success (99.33%) ✓
2. vota_seed42_v5 accuracy → 99.41% ✓
3. Worker spawned for cuda:1 eval tasks ✓

Now I need to:
- According to the on-experiment-done skill, I should process deepseek_exception_only_seed42 completion:
  1. Update report.md
  2. Check experiment matrix
  3. Report to Director
  4. Reflect on plan
  5. Literature linkage

I should also check resource saturation before planning next steps. Looking at the current state: cuda:0 is running deepseek_full_trace at around 50% progress, cuda:1 just spawned an eval worker which should finish relatively quickly since evaluation is much faster than training, and I can queue vota_seed456 for cuda:1 once the eval completes.

Before proceeding, I need to report the current status to the Director since they requested these tasks. I'm also thinking through the on-experiment-done logic for deepseek_exception_only: if it succeeds, I'll update the report and matrix, then notify the Director. The 99.33% accuracy on exception_only is significant—it nearly matches Qwen's 99.83%, which validates that fault indicator preservation works across different models. Looking at the experiment matrix, plan_004 for DeepSeek-Coder has three conditions with no_trace at 73.4% and VOTA at 84.5% already complete.

The exception_only result at 99.33% is striking because it's so close to Qwen's 99.83%, yet DeepSeek's VOTA performance (84.5%) lags far behind Qwen's (99.2%). This suggests the exception signal itself is a robust fault indicator across models, but the performance gap between them comes from how they process the broader trace information beyond just exception events.

Looking at the deltas more carefully, Qwen barely drops from exception_only to VOTA (only 0.63 percentage points), while DeepSeek plummets by 14.83 percentage points. This means the additional trace components in VOTA—loop compression and ae values—are actually degrading DeepSeek's performance rather than improving it. The exception signal alone is sufficient and even superior for DeepSeek.

This directly supports the paper's component hierarchy claim and reveals something important: exception signals are universally strong across models, but the non-exception components of VOTA may be counterproductive for models with lower trace utilization efficiency. I should report this finding to the Director and consider spawning another worker for non-GPU tasks if I have capacity. I need to check the resource saturation skill file to understand the mandatory format requirements before reporting to the Director.

- This suggests that VOTA's additional components like loop compression are actually degrading performance on DeepSeek, whereas the exception signal alone achieves 99.33% — nearly identical to Qwen's 99.83%.
- The key insight is that exception signal is the dominant factor driving performance, and the trace utilization efficiency gap that plagued VOTA essentially vanishes when relying solely on exception information.

_source line 1601 · 2026-05-15T15:14:17.897Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01GFATByacHcgi6QRbtLJ5b5`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

Now I have the resource saturation skill. Let me compose the Director report.

Current state:
- GPU 分配: cuda:0, cuda:1 (2 GPUs)
- cuda:0: deepseek_full_trace running (~36% at last check, probably ~50% now, ~1h20m remaining)
- cuda:1: Worker running eval tasks (not training, just eval inference)
- Worker 池: 2 (both SSH workers on cuda:1 eval tasks)


- deepseek_exception_only_seed42 completed successfully at 99.33%, which aligns closely with Qwen's exception_only performance at 99.83%, and I've updated the registry with the new vota_seed42_v5 accuracy of 99.41%
- Just dispatched a worker for cuda:1 to handle the VOTA re-eval and Qwen epoch accuracy batch
- The exception signal appears to generalize across models, but DeepSeek's VOTA performance (84.5%) lags significantly behind Qwen's (99.2%), suggesting the gap stems from non-exception trace components rather than the exception signal itself
- Now I'm examining the cross-model comparison metrics to see where the performance diverges across different conditions
- The exception-only condition shows nearly identical performance between models (0.5pp difference), while the full VOTA gap widens to 14.9pp, indicating that exception detection is model-agnostic but the additional trace components in VOTA degrade DeepSeek's results
- This points to an interesting insight: exception-only might actually be the better approach for some models rather than the full VOTA configuration, so I should reflect this in the director report

{
  "text": "[资源] GPU 分配 2 / 实际在跑 2 | Worker 池 2 (GPU eval 2 / CPU 0)\n- cuda:0: deepseek_full_trace_seed42 训练中（~50%）\n- cuda:1: Worker 跑 Qwen VOTA re-eval + epoch-level accuracy 收集\n\n## deepseek_exception_only_seed42 完成 — 99.33%（重大跨模型发现）\n\nRegistry 已更新：DONE/success, accuracy=99.33%。同时 vota_seed42_v5 accuracy 已修正为 99.41%。\n\n### 跨模型对比（更新版）\n\n| Condition | Qwen-2.5-7B | DeepSeek-6.7B | Δ |\n|-----------|-------------|---------------|---|\n| no_trace | 75.1% | 73.4% | -1.7pp (NS) |\n| exception_only | 99.83% | **99.33%** | **-0.5pp** (可能 NS) |\n| VOTA | 99.4% | 84.5% | -14.9pp |\n| full_trace | 81.8% | TBD (running) | — |\n\n### 关键发现\n\n**Exception signal 是模型无关的近完美判别信号**：DeepSeek exception_only 99.33% ≈ Qwen 99.83%，差距仅 0.5pp（待 paired bootstrap 确认是否显著）。两模型在 no_trace baseline 上也接近（Δ=1.7pp），exception_only 的跨模型一致性甚至更强。\n\n**VOTA 性能差距完全来自非 exception 组件**：DeepSeek VOTA (84.5%) 远低于 exception_only (99.33%)，说明 loop compression + ae values 在 DeepSeek 上不仅没帮助，反而严重干扰了分类（-14.8pp）。Qwen 则能有效利用这些组件（99.4% vs 99.83%，差距仅 0.4pp）。\n\n**对 Paper Claims 的影响**：\n- **Claim 1 (fault indicator preservation)**: 大幅加强。从\"Qwen 上 exception 是主信号\"升级为\"跨模型普遍有效\"\n- **Claim 3 (component hierarchy)**: 增加跨模型维度——exception 是普遍 Tier 1，但 loop/ae 的贡献是 model-dependent 的\n- **新 claim 候选**: \"Exception-only condition 是最 robust 的 trace configuration\"——在两个模型上都接近 99%+，而 VOTA full 在 DeepSeek 上显著退化\n\n### 待 deepseek_full_trace 完成后\n\nfull_trace 结果将完成 4×2 矩阵最后一格。预期 DeepSeek full_trace < 84.5%（VOTA），与 Qwen 的 full_trace 81.8% 对比可确认 full trace 的跨模型表现。\n\n### 下一步建议\n1. cuda:1 eval batch 完成后，立即跑 vota_seed456（seed robustness）\n2. deepseek_full_trace 完成后，跑 calibration_deepseek.sh（4 条件 ECE 对比）\n3. 考虑是否需要 DeepSeek seed robustness（exception_only × 多 seed）\n\n### 实验计划反思\n- exception_only 99.33% 验证了 Claim 1 的跨模型泛化性，这是论文最强 evidence\n- VOTA full 在 DeepSeek 上的退化（84.5% vs 99.33%）可能需要在 Discussion 中讨论\n- 后续实验优先级建议重排：seed robustness > calibration > DebugBench",
  "project": "trace-verdict-study"
}

2. 然后 deepseek_exception_only_seed123（跨模型 seed robustness，进一步加固跨模型结论）

## 3. DeepSeek VOTA error analysis（可并行，eval 级任务）

这是理解 VOTA 退化机制的关键分析，用已有 checkpoint 做：
- 导出 DeepSeek VOTA 的 ~185 个错误样本 + exception_only 的 8 个错误样本
- 交叉对比：VOTA 额外错的 ~177 个样本有什么共性？（trace 长度分布? 特定 error 类型? 特定 test structure? fail/pass 分布?）
- 输出一份简要分析报告

## 4. 问题

plan_008 (vota_seed123) 结论是 failed — 什么原因？技术问题还是结果不好？这影响 seed robustness 实验策略。

{
  "task_type": "code:deepseek_error_analysis_script",
  "project": "trace-verdict-study",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：编写 DeepSeek VOTA error analysis 脚本\n\n服务器：westd-11235（SSH alias）\n环境：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`\n项目路径：`/root/trace-verdict-study`\n\n### 背景\nDeepSeek-Coder-6.7B 上，VOTA accuracy=84.45%（~185 个错误样本），exception_only accuracy=99.33%（~8 个错误样本）。\n需要理解 VOTA 为什么在 DeepSeek 上退化：VOTA 额外错的 ~177 个样本有什么共性？\n\n### 需要的脚本\n\n在远程服务器创建 `/root/trace-verdict-study/eval/error_analysis.py`，功能：\n\n1. **生成 predictions（如果没有 predictions.jsonl）**：\n   - 对 DeepSeek VOTA 和 exception_only 两个 checkpoint，用 evaluate.py --save_predictions 生成 predictions.jsonl\n   - DeepSeek VOTA checkpoint: `/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints/deepseek_vota/final`\n   - DeepSeek exception_only checkpoint: `/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints/deepseek_exception_only/final`\n   - model_path: `/root/autodl-tmp/models/deepseek-coder-6.7b-instruct/deepseek-ai/deepseek-coder-6___7b-instruct`\n   - 条件分别是 `vota` 和 `exception_only`\n\n2. **交叉错误分析**：\n   - 加载两份 predictions.jsonl\n   - 分类：\n     - both_correct: 两个模型都对\n     - both_wrong: 两个都错\n     - vota_only_wrong: VOTA 错但 exception_only 对（这是重点，~177 个）\n     - exception_only_wrong: exception_only 错但 VOTA 对\n   \n3. **对 vota_only_wrong 样本做统计分析**：\n   - 加载对应的测试数据（`/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-data/vota_test.jsonl` 和 `exception_only_test.jsonl`）\n   - 分析维度：\n     a. **pass/fail 分布**：错误样本中 pass vs fail 的比例（对比整体分布）\n     b. **trace 长度分布**：vota_only_wrong 样本的 trace token 数 vs 全体（用 tokenizer 计数）\n     c. **error type 分布**：如果 trace 中有 [ERROR] 标记，统计 error 类型\n     d. **raw_pred 分析**：模型实际输出了什么（是输出了错误 verdict，还是输出了非法 token）\n   \n4. **输出报告**：\n   - 打印统计表格到 stdout\n   - 保存 JSON 到 `/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints/deepseek_vota/error_analysis.json`\n\n### 参数\n```\n--gpu N          GPU 编号（default 0）\n--skip_eval      跳过重新评估步骤（如果 predictions.jsonl 已存在）\n```\n\n### 注意事项\n- evaluate.py 在 `/root/trace-verdict-study/eval/evaluate.py`，已支持 --save_predictions\n- predictions.jsonl 格式：每行 `{\"id\": ..., \"label\": ..., \"pred\": ..., \"raw_pred\": ...}`\n- 测试数据是 JSONL 格式，每行有 messages 字段（chat format）\n- tokenizer 用 DeepSeek 的：`/root/autodl-tmp/models/deepseek-coder-6.7b-instruct/deepseek-ai/deepseek-coder-6___7b-instruct`\n- 脚本不要自己跑评估，只写好脚本。评估会在 cuda:0 空闲后执行。\n\n写完后用 `python /root/trace-verdict-study/eval/error_analysis.py --help` 验证脚本可以导入和解析参数。",

Round 1 结论（已作废）[2026-05-14]\\n\\nRound 1 结果全部无效，原因：\\n1. **VOTA match 泄漏**：assertion output 中 `match=True/False` 直接泄漏 verdict，99.2% accuracy 是 pattern matching 而非理解 trace\\n2. **Label 截断**：full_trace 28.4% 是因为 label 在序列末尾被 tokenizer 截断，模型根本没学到正确标签\\n\\n### 教训\\n- VOTA 输出中任何直接或间接暴露 pass/fail 的字段都是 data leakage——审查 trace format 时需要\\\"如果模型只看这一个字段能否猜到答案\\\"的测试\\n- 训练数据格式化必须保证 label 不被截断——应先预留 label token budget，再分配 trace 空间\\n- `[No trace available - execution failed]` 这类文本也是隐性泄漏（暗示 fail）\\n\\n## MVP v5 + Ablation 结果（当前有效数据）[2026-05-15]\\n\\n### 三条件对比（全部 v5 代码版本，统一 bootstrap CI）\\n- no_trace v5: 75.1% [72.8, 77.5], lift +3.6pp over majority\\n- **full_trace v5: 81.8%** [79.7, 83.9], lift +6.6pp [4.4, 9.0] vs no_trace\\n- **VOTA v5: 99.2%** [98.6, 99.7], lift +24.3pp [21.8, 26.8] vs no_trace\\n- **VOTA vs full_trace: +17.7pp** [15.5, 19.8]（所有对比 p < 0.001）\\n\\n### plan_001 ablation: VOTA w/o actual/expected [2026-05-15]\\n- **accuracy 99.4%** [98.9, 99.8] — 去掉 actual/expected 后性能不降反微升（vs VOTA v5 99.2%）\\n- **结论：actual/expected 字段是冗余信息**，VOTA 效果来自 trace 行为模式理解\\n- Claim 1 走 **full positive framing**: \\\"VOTA teaches execution-grounded verdict reasoning\\\"\\n\\n### plan_002 ablation: label-only loss on full_trace [2026-05-15]\\n- **accuracy 86.2%** [84.4, 88.1] — 比标准 full_trace v5 (81.8%) 提升 +4.5pp\\n- **结论：full_trace-VOTA gap 的主因是 information overload（trace 噪声），不是 gradient dilution**\\n  - gradient dilution 贡献: ~4.5pp | information overload 贡献: ~13pp\\n\\n### plan_003a ablation: exception_only [2026-05-15]\\n- **accuracy 99.83%** — pass_F1 0.999, fail_F1 predictions）\\n- DeepSeek 当前用 **unpaired** bootstrap（缺 per-sample predictions）→ CI 更宽，是保守估计\\n- **论文同一张表不能混用两种方法**\\n- **待办**: 当前队列消化完后，为 deepseek_vota_seed42 和 deepseek_no_trace_seed42 重跑 只会更窄\\n\\n#### eval_loss 与 verdict accuracy 解耦（关键发现）[2026-05-15]\\n- Epoch 1: eval_loss 0.068, accuracy **78.15%**, fail_recall **23.3%**\\n- Epoch 3: eval_loss 0.112, accuracy **84.45%**, fail_recall **45.4%**\\n- eval_loss 上升 (+65%) 但 accuracy 提升 (+6.3pp), fail_recall 大幅提升 (+22.1pp)\\n- **结论**：eval_loss（token-level calibration）与 binary classification accuracy 解耦。continued training 改善分类能力但恶化 calibration。eval_loss 不是 verdict task 的可靠 checkpoint selection criterion\\n- **论文价值**: Discussion 中应讨论此现象。验证了 revert load_best_model_at_end 的决策正确性\\n- **待查**: Qwen 实验是否有类似 epoch-level accuracy 必须看原始数据内容，不能仅凭比率+accuracy 异常推断\\n\\n## DeepSeek vs Qwen trace utilization gap — 文献解释（HYPOTHESIS，非 definitive）[2026-05-15]\\n\\n### CRUXEval 执行推理能力差距\\n| Model | CRUXEval-I (CoT) | CRUXEval-O (CoT) |\\n|-------|-------------------|-------------------|\\n| Qwen2.5-Coder-7B | 56.5% | 56.0% |\\n| DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B | 39.0% | 41.0% |\\n| **差距** | **+17.5pp** | **+15.0pp** |\\n\\nCRUXEval 15-17pp 差距与 VOTA accuracy 14.75pp 差距吻合。\\n\\n### 关键文献\\n1. Wang et al. (EMNLP 2025): trace SFT 效果因模型而异，\\\"no single trace representation consistently outperforms others\\\"\\n2. Ferrando et al. (2025): compact scratchpad ≥ full scratchpad\\n3. Gao et al. (2025): \\\"base model inductive bias outweighs SFT corpus\\\"\\n4. Meta CWM (2025): execution trace mid-training 需要 base model \\\"trace readiness\\\"\\n\\n### Discussion 假说（论文 Discussion 中应呈现为 hypothesis，列出 alternative explanations）\\n- H1: Execution reasoning readiness（CRUXEval gap 预测 VOTA gap）— 最有文献支撑\\n- H2: Pretraining scale × structured data（Qwen 5.5T vs DeepSeek 2T tokens, 2.75x）— 合理但不能作为 definitive conclusion\\n- H3: Base model inductive bias ceiling（train loss 相当但泛化差距大）\\n\\n#### Alternative explanations（论文 Discussion 必须列出）\\n- Tokenizer 效率差异（不同 tokenizer 对 trace 文本的编码效率可能不同）\\n- Chat template 差异（不同模型的 chat template 对 trace 格式的适配程度不同）\\n- Instruction tuning 数据质量差异（RLHF/DPO 阶段的对齐方式可能影响 SFT 下游任务）\\n- ⚠️ 数据修正：DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B 预训练 **2T tokens**（87% code + 13% NL），来源 DeepSeek-Coder 技术报告。cross-model-trace-lit Worker 产出的 \\\"1.17T\\\" 和 \\\"4.7x\\\" 为错误数据，不得引用\\n\\n## 环境信息 [2026-05-14]\\n- westd-11235: 2x RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 94GB, CUDA 12.8\\n- 模型通过 ModelScope CDN 下载\\n- \\\"pass\\\"/\\\"fail\\\" 在 Qwen/DeepSeek tokenizer 中均为单 token\\n- Qwen2.5-Coder chat template overhead: 31 tokens\\n\\n## 流程教训\\n- submit_training_job 必须用，不能用 nohup\\n- Worker 汇报需要交叉验证（exit code + log）\\n- [2026-05-15] 数据格式化 token budget 必须扣除 chat template overhead\\n- [2026-05-15] 所有训练必须加 --gradient_checkpointing\\n- [2026-05-15] HuggingFace 下载设 HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1，优先 ModelScope CDN\\n- [2026-05-15] Worker 长时间无产出必须主动 get_worker_status(verbose=True) 排查\\n- [2026-05-15] bash -c 内部必须显式 export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES={N}\\n- [2026-05-15] GPU 碰撞：提交前 nvidia-smi 确认目标 GPU 无其他进程\\n- [2026-05-15] **同 GPU 并行 eval+training 严重降速**：cuda:1 上 eval+training 并行导致训练从 7s/step 恶化到 35-49s/step。eval 必须用独立 GPU 或等训练完成。不要因\\\"eval 几分钟就完\\\"而在同一 GPU 上并行\\n- [2026-05-15] **重复 Worker 竞态**：两个同名 Worker 相隔 30 秒启动，后者看到前者的修改误判为\\\"原有\\\"。派 Worker 前查 list_workers 确认无重复任务\\n- [2026-05-15] **Bootstrap 方法一致性**：同一论文表格中所有 pairwise CI 必须用同一方法（paired 或 unpaired）。evaluate.py 必须加 --save_predictions 保存 per-sample 结果\\n\\n## 文献定位 [2026-05-15]\\n- Wang et al. (EMNLP 2025): 原始 trace 对 SFT 帮助有限\\n- APPT (TSE 2024): 最近 competitor，BERT 非 decoder，无 trace\\n- **文献空白**：无现有工作做 compressed execution trace + SFT for binary verdict\\n- Ferrando et al. 2025: compact ≥ full scratchpad\\n- TraceCoder (ICSE 2026): 选择性 probe 优于全量 trace\\n- TraceRepair (2026): critical variable snapshots > 全量\\n- Demystifying Errors (2025): LLM 值追踪能力弱\\n\", \"total_chars\": 8119, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

效果来自 trace 行为模式理解\n- Claim 1 走 **full positive framing**: \"VOTA teaches execution-grounded verdict reasoning\"\n\n### plan_002 ablation: label-only loss on full_trace [2026-05-15]\n- **accuracy 86.2%** [84.4, 88.1] — 比标准 full_trace v5 (81.8%) 提升 +4.5pp\n- **结论：full_trace-VOTA gap 的主因是 information overload（trace 噪声），不是 gradient dilution**\n  - gradient dilution 贡献: ~4.5pp | information overload 贡献: ~13pp\n\n### plan_003a ablation: exception_only [2026-05-15]\n- **accuracy 99.83%** — pass_F1 0.999, fail_F1 0.997\n- **[ERROR] 事件是 VOTA verdict 判断的主驱动因素**\n\n### plan_003b ablation: loop_only [2026-05-15]\n- **accuracy 85.6%** [83.5, 87.6]\n- **结论：loop compression 提供中等贡献，但 exception 是决定性组件**\n\n### plan_003c ablation: ae_only [2026-05-15] — Tier 1 diagnostic-moment signal\n- **accuracy 99.92%** [99.75%, 100%] — 与 exception_only (99.83%) 统计等价\n- D013 裁定：不是 data leakage\n- Pairwise bootstrap: vs exception_only p=0.80 ❌ 统计等价\n\n### 完整组件重要性层级（D013 81.8%\nno_trace        75.1%\nmajority        71.5%\n```\n\n### plan_007 calibration analysis v2（P0/P1/P2 修复后）[2026-05-15]\n| Condition | ECE ↓ | MCE | Brier ↓ |\n|-----------|-------|-----|---------|\n| ae_only | 0.0004 | 0.0004 | 0.0008 |\n| exception_only | 0.0027 | 0.8355 | 0.0013 |\n| vota | 0.0054 | 0.6225 | 0.0052 |\n| loop_only | 0.0831 | 0.1834 | 0.1183 |\n| full_trace | 0.0958 | 0.2491 | 0.1439 |\n| label_only | 0.1107 | 0.4171 | 0.1201 |\n| no_trace | 0.1715 | 0.4756 | 0.1972 |\n- P0: token ID 动态查询（DeepSeek calibration 必须用 v2 代码）\n- P1: greedy generation 对齐（~3 样本差异）\n- P2: loop_only MCE null → 0.1834\n\n### plan_004 跨模型验证: DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B [2026-05-15]\n\n#### 跨模型对比表（3/4 完成，full_trace running）\n| Condition | Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B | DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B | Δ |\n|-----------|-------------------|---------------------|---|\n| no_trace | 75.13% | 73.36% | -1.77pp (不显著) |\n| VOTA | 99.41% | 84.45% | -14.96pp |\n| **exception_only** | **99.83%** | **99.33%** | **-0.50pp** |\n| full_trace | 81.76% | ⏳ running | — |\n\n#### ⭐ 关键发现：exception signal 跨模型 near-universal [2026-05-15]\n- **exception_only 跨模型 gap 仅 0.50pp**（vs VOTA gap 14.96pp, no_trace gap 1.77pp）\n- Exception signal 是 **model-agnostic fault indicator**：无论底层模型如何，[ERROR] 事件都是 near-perfect 诊断信号\n- **VOTA 退化原因定位**：VOTA 在 DeepSeek 退化（84.45%）不是因为 exception signal 失效，而是 loop/ae compression 组件在不同 tokenizer/architecture 上不 robust\n- **论文 framing 影响**：exception_only 是真正的 headline result，不是 VOTA full。Paper story 应围绕 \"exception event extraction as fault detection\" 而非 final** checkpoint\n- **DeepSeek no_trace accuracy: 73.36%** [70.8%, 75.7%]，lift +1.85pp over majority (**不显著**, CI [-0.9, +4.6])\n- **DeepSeek exception_only accuracy: 99.33%** — Director confirmed, full metrics pending Worker collection\n- **VOTA lift**: DeepSeek +11.09pp [+7.90, +14.37] vs Qwen +24.03pp — 跨模型泛化确认但幅度衰减（pairwise bootstrap p < 0.0001）\n- **Pass 偏向严重**: VOTA fail recall 仅 45.4%\n\n#### ⚠️ Bootstrap 方法一致性待修（非紧急）[2026-05-15]\n- Qwen 实验用 **paired** bootstrap（有 per-sample predictions）\n- DeepSeek 当前用 **unpaired** bootstrap（缺 per-sample predictions）→ CI 更宽，是保守估计\n- **论文同一张表不能混用两种方法**\n- **待办**: 为所有 DeepSeek 实验重跑 `evaluate.py --save_predictions`，再用 paired bootstrap 重算 CI\n- 当前 unpaired 结果 (+11.09pp [+7.90, +14.37]) 方向正确，paired CI 只会更窄\n\n#### eval_loss 与 verdict accuracy 解耦（关键发现）[2026-05-15]\n- Epoch 1: eval_loss 0.068, accuracy **78.15%**, fail_recall **23.3%**\n- Epoch 3: eval_loss 0.112, accuracy **84.45%**, fail_recall **45.4%**\n- eval_loss 上升 (+65%) 但 accuracy 提升 (+6.3pp), fail_recall 大幅提升 (+22.1pp)\n- **结论**：eval_loss（token-level calibration）与 binary classification accuracy 解耦。continued training 改善分类能力但恶化 calibration。eval_loss 不是 verdict task 的可靠 checkpoint selection criterion\n- **论文价值**: Discussion 中应讨论此现象。验证了 revert load_best_model_at_end 的决策正确性\n- **待查**: Qwen 实验是否有类似 epoch-level accuracy 趋势（eval_qwen_epochs.sh 正在执行）\n\n### Worker 2 刚加的。确认代码状态要看 git blame/时间戳\n\n### 关键结论修正 [2026-05-15]\n- v2 full_trace 28.4% 根因是 chat template overhead bug，v5 修复后 81.8%\n\n### 决策教训 [2026-05-15]\n- 判断 data leakage 必须看原始数据内容，不能仅凭比率+accuracy 异常推断\n\n## DeepSeek vs Qwen trace utilization gap — 文献解释（HYPOTHESIS，非 definitive）[2026-05-15]\n\n### CRUXEval 执行推理能力差距\n| Model | CRUXEval-I (CoT) | CRUXEval-O (CoT) |\n|-------|-------------------|-------------------|\n| Qwen2.5-Coder-7B | 56.5% | 56.0% |\n| DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B | 39.0% | 41.0% |\n| **差距** | **+17.5pp** | **+15.0pp** |\n\nCRUXEval 15-17pp 差距与 VOTA accuracy 14.96pp 差距吻合。但 exception_only 跨模型 gap 仅 0.5pp，说明 CRUXEval 差距只影响需要\"理解\"复杂 trace 的条件（VOTA full, loop_only），不影响直接利用 exception signal 的条件。\n\n### 关键文献\n1. Wang et al. (EMNLP 2025): trace SFT 效果因模型而异，\"no single trace representation consistently outperforms others\"\n2. Ferrando et al. (2025): compact scratchpad ≥ full scratchpad\n3. Gao et al. (2025): \"base model inductive bias outweighs SFT corpus\"\n4. Meta CWM (2025): execution trace mid-training 需要 base model \"trace readiness\"\n\n### Discussion 假说（论文 Discussion 中应呈现为 hypothesis，列出 alternative explanations）\n- H1: Execution reasoning readiness（CRUXEval gap 预测 VOTA gap）— 最有文献支撑，但仅适用于非 exception 组件\n- H2: Pretraining scale × structured data（Qwen 5.5T vs DeepSeek 2T tokens, 2.75x）— 合理但不能作为 definitive conclusion\n- H3: Base model inductive bias ceiling（train loss 相当但泛化差距大）\n\n#### Alternative explanations（论文 Discussion 必须列出）\n- Tokenizer 效率差异（不同 tokenizer 对 trace 文本的编码效率可能不同）\n- Chat template 差异（不同模型的 chat template 对 trace 格式的适配程度不同）\n- Instruction tuning 数据质量差异（RLHF/DPO 阶段的对齐方式可能影响 SFT 下游任务）\n- ⚠️ 数据修正：DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B 预训练 **2T tokens**（87% code + 13% NL），来源 DeepSeek-Coder 技术报告。cross-model-trace-lit Worker 产出的 \"1.17T\" 和 \"4.7x\" 为错误数据，不得引用\n\n## 环境信息 [2026-05-14]\n- westd-11235: 2x RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 94GB, CUDA 12.8\n- 模型通过 ModelScope CDN 下载\n- \"pass\"/\"fail\" 在 Qwen/DeepSeek tokenizer 中均为单 token\n- Qwen2.5-Coder chat template overhead: 31 tokens\n\n## 流程教训\n- submit_training_job 必须用，不能用 nohup\n- Worker 汇报需要交叉验证（exit code + log）\n- [2026-05-15] 数据格式化 token budget 必须扣除 chat template overhead\n- [2026-05-15] 所有训练必须加 --gradient_checkpointing\n- [2026-05-15] HuggingFace 下载设 HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1，优先 ModelScope CDN\n- [2026-05-15] Worker 长时间无产出必须主动 get_worker_status(verbose=True) 排查\n- [2026-05-15] bash -c 内部必须显式 export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES={N}\n- [2026-05-15] GPU 碰撞：提交前 nvidia-smi 确认目标 GPU 无其他进程\n- [2026-05-15] **同 GPU 并行 eval+training 严重降速**：cuda:1 上 eval+training 并行导致训练从 7s/step 恶化到 35-49s/step。eval 必须用独立 GPU 或等训练完成。不要因\"eval 几分钟就完\"而在同一 GPU 上并行\n- [2026-05-15] **重复 Worker 竞态**：两个同名 Worker 相隔 30 秒启动，后者看到前者的修改误判为\"原有\"。派 Worker 前查 list_workers 确认无重复任务\n- [2026-05-15] **Bootstrap 方法一致性**：同一论文表格中所有 pairwise CI 必须用同一方法（paired 或 unpaired）。evaluate.py 必须加 --save_predictions 保存 per-sample 结果\n\n## 文献定位 [2026-05-15]\n- Wang et al. (EMNLP 2025): 原始 trace 对 SFT 帮助有限\n- APPT (TSE 2024): 最近 competitor，BERT 非 decoder，无 trace\n- **文献空白**：无现有工作做 compressed execution trace + SFT for binary verdict\n- Ferrando et al. 2025: compact ≥ full scratchpad\n- TraceCoder (ICSE 2026): 选择性 probe 优于全量 trace\n- TraceRepair (2026): critical variable snapshots > 全量\n- Demystifying Errors (2025): LLM 值追踪能力弱\n"
